The preview is used for exactly three things: (1) `renderer.stage.prewarm(vfxIdsIn(preview.events))` so shaders/particles for the likely VFX are resident before the server message lands; (2) `expectedDurationMs = buildTimeline(preview.events).durationMs` to size the input-lock and the "waiting" indicator; (3) `digestBattleState(preview.state)` compared with `battleEvents.stateDigest`. **No preview frame is ever rendered**: `TimelinePlayer.play` is only ever called with a timeline built from `m.events`. On mismatch: `console.warn('battle desync', battleId, turn)`, `ui.battle.desyncs += 1`, `send('battleResync', { battleId })`, and the server's events are still played (they are authoritative regardless). The `battleEvents.rng` field replaces the local `authoritative.rng` after every turn so the next preview starts from the server's stream.

**HP is never computed locally.** The HUD bar targets are `damage.hpAfter`/`heal.hpAfter`; `authoritative.sides[i].party[j].creature.hp` is overwritten from those same fields as events are applied.

### 20.5.6 Battle menu (DOM) and in-canvas elements

DOM (`BattleOverlay`, bottom 28 % of the viewport, `Panel` without title): root menu `Fight · Party · Bag · Orbs · Run/Forfeit` (hotkeys `1–5`), moves grid of 4 `MoveButton`s (hotkeys `1–4`, `Escape` back), party list of `CreatureCard variant="row"` (switch), bag list filtered by `usableInBattle`, orbs list. Disabled reasons come from `validateAction` (`ActionError` → label table, e.g. `noPp → 'No PP'`, `choiceLocked → 'Choice-locked'`, `trapped → 'Trapped'`). The last 3 log lines render in the DOM panel's left third with `--t-body-lg`.

In-canvas (render `HudLayer`, spine Pillar 8: never covered by an effect): both active battlers' HP bars anchored above the billboards with name, level, HP numbers (own side only), status glyph, stage arrows (▲/▼ per stat with count); the turn label; the orb shake counter. The `BattleResultCard` (DOM modal) shows `RewardsSummary` and, for `battleEnd` with `evolveReady`/`learnMove` events in the last turn, the `evolve`/`learnMove` modals queued in order, each an explicit REST call (`POST /creature/:id/evolve`, `POST /creature/:id/moves`) — never automatic.

---

## §20.6 IN-WORLD HUD

`HudLayer` (render) draws in-canvas at the end of the frame with the token fonts. The client owns `HudWriter`, which keeps a `HudModel` and calls `renderer.hud.set(partial)` with **only the fields that changed since the last flush** (a per-field dirty bitmask; `flush()` is a no-op when the mask is 0 — this is the "HUD dirty-flag" rule of §20.10).

```ts
// packages/client/src/scene/HudWriter.ts
export interface HudModel {
  readonly party: readonly { readonly speciesId: string; readonly variantKey: VariantKey; readonly hpFrac: number; readonly status: string | null; readonly fainted: boolean }[]; // ≤ 6; top-left strip, 48 px thumbs
  readonly zoneName: string | null;            // display face 48 px, centre-top, banner timing §20.2.6; null after the banner
  readonly timeOfDay: number;                  // 0..1 -> sun/moon glyph + hh:mm in the top-right
  readonly questHint: string | null;           // one line ≤ 60 chars, below the time; from the active quest's first undone objective label
  readonly chat: readonly { readonly name: string; readonly text: string; readonly at: number }[]; // last 6 lines, bottom-left, each fades 8 s after `at`
  readonly prompt: { readonly key: string; readonly verb: string; readonly target: string } | null; // "E — Talk to Fenkeeper", bottom-centre
  readonly nameplates: readonly { readonly id: string; readonly name: string; readonly guildTag: string; readonly inBattle: boolean }[]; // ≤ 16 nearest; render positions them over actors
  readonly battle: { readonly bars: readonly { readonly who: Ref; readonly name: string; readonly level: number; readonly hp: number; readonly maxHp: number; readonly status: string | null; readonly stages: string; readonly showNumbers: boolean }[]; readonly turnLabel: string; readonly shakes: number } | null;
  readonly connection: 'connected' | 'reconnecting' | 'offline';
  readonly dev: { readonly fps: number; readonly drawCalls: number; readonly gpuMs: number; readonly desyncs: number; readonly rubberBands: number } | null; // F3 in dev builds
}
```

Write cadence: `party` on `applyInventoryDelta` (store subscription → writer field, not React); `zoneName` on `loadZone`; `timeOfDay` at ≤ 1 Hz (rounded to 1/1440 of a day, so the glyph changes once per in-game minute); `questHint` on `applyProgressionDelta`; `chat` on each chat message; `prompt` only when the nearest target id changes; `nameplates` every 500 ms with the nearest-N sort; `battle` from `TimelinePlayer` frames; `dev` at 1 Hz. **DOM is used only for menus, panels, modals, the dialogue box, the chat input and toasts.** No DOM node is created, removed or restyled from `World3D.tick`.

---

## §20.7 AUDIO

### 20.7.1 Graph

```
AudioBufferSourceNode (stem) ─┐
AudioBufferSourceNode (stem) ─┼─> GainNode(stem) ─> GainNode(music) ─────────────────────────────┐
...                            ┘                                                                    │
AudioBufferSourceNode (sfx) ─> GainNode(voice) ─> PannerNode(HRTF) ─┬─> GainNode(sfx) ───────────┼─> GainNode(master) ─> DynamicsCompressorNode ─> destination
                                                                    └─> GainNode(reverbSend) ─> ConvolverNode(zoneIR) ─> GainNode(sfx)
AudioBufferSourceNode (ui) ─> GainNode(ui) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

All nodes are standard WebAudio (`AudioContext`, `GainNode`, `PannerNode`, `ConvolverNode`, `DynamicsCompressorNode`, `AudioBufferSourceNode`); no library. The context is created on first user gesture (`pointerdown`/`keydown`, once) and `resume()`d; before that every `play()` is a no-op.

### 20.7.2 Mixer table — every value the engine ships

| Bus / parameter | Value |
|---|---|
| master | 0 dB (gain 1.0); compressor threshold −12 dB, knee 6, ratio 4, attack 0.003 s, release 0.25 s |
| music | −6 dB (0.501) |
| sfx | −3 dB (0.708) |
| ui | −8 dB (0.398) |
| duck target (music while `ui.dialogue !== null` or a modal is open) | −9 dB relative; attack 250 ms, release 600 ms, linear ramps |
| battle music swap | world theme fades out 400 ms, battle theme fades in 400 ms, crossfaded; victory cue plays on the ui bus |
| stem `threat` | gain 0 in world, 1 in battle (400 ms ramp); 1 in raid phase ≥ 1 |
| stem `night` | gain = `smoothstep(0.78, 0.82, t) * (1 - smoothstep(0.20, 0.24, t))` over `timeOfDay t`, wrapping so night is `[0.80, 0.22)`; ≈ 115 real seconds per ramp |
| stem `biome` | 1 in the zone's own biome theme; 0 during the 400 ms handover crossfade |
| stem `base` | 1 |
| harvested zone | every stem low-passed at 1 200 Hz (`BiquadFilterNode`, Q 0.7) and `night` forced 0 — the audio equivalent of the flat-shaded look |
| panner | `panningModel 'HRTF'`, `distanceModel 'inverse'`, `refDistance 4`, `maxDistance 60`, `rolloffFactor 1.5`, cone omnidirectional |
| listener | position = camera position; forward = camera forward; up = (0,1,0); updated once per rendered frame |
| reverb send | outdoor 0.12, town plaza 0.18, interior (gym/shop/healer) 0.35, cave/shrine/rift 0.50; IR = algorithmic exponentially decaying white noise, 0.5 s outdoor / 1.2 s interior / 2.4 s cave, generated once at boot into `AudioBuffer`s (no IR files) |
| polyphony | 24 simultaneous sfx voices; per-id cooldown 40 ms; per-id concurrency ≤ 4; the quietest (by distance) voice is stolen |
| ui sfx | non-positional; hover 0, click −14 dB, error −10 dB, toast −12 dB |
| loudness target | stems −16 LUFS integrated, sfx peaks ≤ −3 dBTP (enforced by `40-pipelines.md`'s normalise step; the engine trusts the manifest `lufs`) |

### 20.7.3 `AudioDirector`

```ts
// packages/client/src/audio/AudioDirector.ts
export interface AudioDirector {
  init(manifest: AudioManifest): Promise<void>;          // decodes ui + the current zone's sfx set; music stems stream-decoded lazily per theme
  setTheme(themeId: string, crossfadeMs: number): void;  // world theme per zone; 'battle' | 'gym' | 'elite' | 'champion' | 'raid' by format/opponent
  setThreat(on: boolean): void; setNight(t01: number): void; setHarvested(on: boolean): void;
  duck(on: boolean): void;
  play(sfxId: string, at: { x: number; y: number; z: number } | null): void;   // null = non-positional (ui bus if id starts with 'ui_', else sfx bus centred)
  setListener(x: number, y: number, z: number, fx: number, fy: number, fz: number): void;
  setBus(bus: 'master' | 'music' | 'sfx' | 'ui', gain01: number): void;        // from SettingsState.audio, linear 0..1 → applied as gain^2 (perceptual)
  setReverbZone(kind: 'outdoor' | 'plaza' | 'interior' | 'cave'): void;
  step(): void;                                                                  // per fixed step: ramps, voice GC, stem loop scheduling 200 ms ahead
  suspend(): void; resume(): void;                                               // document.hidden → suspend after 2 s; music only, sfx already stop
}
```

Stems loop with two alternating `AudioBufferSourceNode`s scheduled from `loopStartSec`/`loopEndSec` 200 ms ahead of the loop point (no `loop = true` on the source, so the four stems stay sample-aligned). All four stems of a theme start at the same `context.currentTime + 0.05`.

### 20.7.4 Asset manifest — the shape the client consumes (`40-pipelines.md` emits it as `public/audio/manifest.json`)

```ts
// packages/client/src/audio/manifest.ts
export interface AudioClipRef { readonly url: string; readonly urlFallback: string; readonly durationSec: number; readonly bytes: number; readonly hash: string } // url = .webm (Opus 96 kb/s), urlFallback = .m4a (AAC 128 kb/s); chosen once via HTMLMediaElement.canPlayType('audio/webm; codecs=opus')
export interface StemRef extends AudioClipRef { readonly loopStartSec: number; readonly loopEndSec: number; readonly lufs: number }
export interface AudioManifest {
  readonly version: 1;
  readonly builtAt: string;                                                      // ISO-8601
  readonly themes: Readonly<Record<string, { readonly stems: { readonly base: StemRef; readonly threat: StemRef; readonly biome: StemRef; readonly night: StemRef }; readonly bpm: number }>>; // theme ids: one per biome (11) + battle, gym, elite, champion, raid, victory
  readonly sfx: Readonly<Record<string, { readonly clips: readonly AudioClipRef[]; readonly gainDb: number; readonly bus: 'sfx' | 'ui'; readonly maxConcurrent: number }>>; // clips.length 1..4 round-robin variants
  readonly ambience: Readonly<Record<string, StemRef>>;                          // per-biome bed, looped on the music bus at −12 dB
  readonly zoneThemes: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;                         // ZoneId -> theme id; all 21 present (validated at client boot in dev)
}
```

Boot validation (dev only): every `zoneThemes` key is a `ZoneId`; every `MOVE_ANIMS` / `DEFAULT_BY_TYPE` `sfx` id and every `sfx` id the timeline can emit (`hit_*`, `faint`, `switch`, `orb_*`, `status_*`, `stage_up`, `stage_down`, `weather_*`, `ui_*`) exists in `manifest.sfx`. Missing → `console.error` list, boot continues silently for those ids.

`loopStartSec`/`loopEndSec` already include the Opus pre-skip correction (`40-pipelines.md` §40.4.5); the client schedules them against the decoded buffer as-is and applies no offset of its own. Stems are 16 bars by default (32 for `battle`/`champion`, 8 for `raid`/`victory`), so a theme loop is 16–64 s. <!-- reconciled 2026-09-18: 40-pipelines.md OQ-5 -->

---

## §20.8 SETTINGS AND ACCESSIBILITY

### 20.8.1 `SettingsState` (persisted; `persist({ name: 'ewa.settings', version: 1, storage: createJSONStorage(() => localStorage) })`)

```ts
export type Colorblind = 'none' | 'deuteranopia' | 'protanopia' | 'tritanopia';
export type InputAction = 'forward' | 'back' | 'left' | 'right' | 'run' | 'interact' | 'jump' | 'chat' | 'emote1' | 'emote2' | 'emote3' | 'emote4' | 'emote5' | 'emote6' | 'challenge' | 'rotateLeft' | 'rotateRight' | 'party' | 'bag' | 'journal' | 'pause' | 'toggleHud';
export interface SettingsState {
  readonly graphics: { readonly reducedEffects: boolean; readonly renderScale: 0.75 | 1; readonly dof: boolean; readonly bloom: boolean; readonly grain: boolean; readonly chroma: boolean; readonly shake: boolean; readonly hitstop: boolean; readonly slowmo: boolean };
  readonly accessibility: { readonly reducedMotion: 'system' | 'on' | 'off'; readonly colorblind: Colorblind; readonly uiScale: number /* 0.9..1.4 step 0.05 */; readonly battleTextSpeed: 'normal' | 'fast' | 'instant'; readonly screenFlash: boolean };
  readonly audio: { readonly master: number; readonly music: number; readonly sfx: number; readonly ui: number; readonly muteWhenHidden: boolean }; // 0..1
  readonly keybinds: Readonly<Record<InputAction, readonly string[]>>;         // KeyboardEvent.code values, 1..2 per action
  readonly social: { readonly showChat: boolean; readonly allowChallenges: boolean; readonly allowTrades: boolean; readonly allowDmsFrom: 'everyone' | 'friends' };
  set<K extends keyof SettingsState>(k: K, v: Partial<SettingsState[K]>): void;
  resetKeybinds(): void;
}
```

### 20.8.2 Effective reduced effects — accessibility always wins

```ts
export function effectiveReduced(s: SettingsState, systemPrefersReduced: boolean): boolean {
  return s.accessibility.reducedMotion === 'on' || (s.accessibility.reducedMotion === 'system' && systemPrefersReduced) || s.graphics.reducedEffects;
}
export function effectsFlags(s: SettingsState, reduced: boolean): EffectsFlags {
  return reduced
    ? { dof: false, bloom: s.graphics.bloom, grain: false, chroma: false, shake: false, hitstop: false, slowmo: false, taa: true }
    : { dof: s.graphics.dof, bloom: s.graphics.bloom, grain: s.graphics.grain, chroma: s.graphics.chroma, shake: s.graphics.shake, hitstop: s.graphics.hitstop, slowmo: s.graphics.slowmo, taa: true };
}
```

`systemPrefersReduced` comes from `matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')` with a `change` listener; a change re-applies immediately (`renderer.setEffects`, `:root[data-reduced]`, `TimelineCtx.reduced`). Bloom stays available under reduced motion because it is static (Pillar 8 lists grain, aberration, shake, hitstop). Graphics toggles for `dof/grain/chroma/shake/hitstop/slowmo` render disabled with the caption "Off: reduced motion" while `reduced` is true. Test: with `reducedMotion: 'on'` the 600-frame capture of spine Pillar 8 shows zero shake offsets and zero `timeScale !== 1` frames.

### 20.8.3 Colourblind palettes for `TypeBadge` (and the sprite rim through the descriptor)

Three full 19-entry alternates in `tokens.json` under `typeColors.deuteranopia|protanopia|tritanopia`, chosen so every adjacent pair in `TYPE_NAMES` order differs by ≥ 40 in CIELAB ΔE under the respective simulation (checked by `gen-tokens.ts` with the Machado 2009 matrices; the build fails otherwise). Independent of colour, the badge always shows `TYPE_GLYPHS[type]`, and the effectiveness chevrons in `MoveButton` are shape-coded (`▲▲ ▲ ▼ ▼▼ ✕`), so no gameplay information is colour-only.

### 20.8.4 UI scale

`accessibility.uiScale` sets `document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--ui-scale', v)` (the one permitted programmatic style write, in `applySettings.ts`) and `renderer.hud.setScale(v)`. Every `--t-*` token is `calc(<px>/16 * 1rem * var(--ui-scale))`. At 1.4 on 1280×720 the panel width formula (§20.2.3) still leaves ≥ 160 px of world visible; the Settings screen previews the scale live.

### 20.8.5 Key remapping

The Settings › Controls table lists every `InputAction` with up to two bindings. Rebinding captures the next `keydown` `code` (`Escape` cancels; `Backspace` clears). Conflicts are rejected with the caption "Already bound to <action>". `Escape`, `Enter`, `Tab`, `F1`–`F12` other than `F1`/`F3`, and browser-reserved combinations are not bindable. `resetKeybinds()` restores the §20.4.5 table. Bindings are stored as `code` strings so layouts (AZERTY) work by physical position; the label shown uses `KeyboardEvent.key` from the capture event.

### 20.8.6 Other accessibility rules

- Every interactive element is reachable by `Tab` in DOM order; panels trap focus while open and return it on close; modals are `role="dialog"` with `aria-modal`, `aria-labelledby`.
- `screenFlash: false` removes the white impact `flash` frames' screen component (the billboard flash remains).
- `battleTextSpeed: 'instant'` sets `LOG_DWELL_MIN` to 0 and `HP_DRAIN` to 0 without touching any other frame.
- Chat and DM text render at `--t-body` minimum; usernames never below `--t-caption`.
- No information is conveyed by motion alone; every animated change also leaves a static result (HP number, log line, badge).

---

## §20.9 SCREENS

Each screen: information hierarchy in one paragraph, then its states. All `/play/*` screens render as the anchored right panel (§20.2.3) over the live world.

**20.9.1 Auth** (`/login`, `/register`). Full-bleed static key-art (`public/keyart/mirevale.ktx2`-derived JPEG, 1440p) with a 420 px `Card` centred-right. Primary: username + password (+ email + starter picker on register). The starter picker is three `CreatureCard variant="showcase"` for `bramblecub / glimfin / cindercap` with type badges and a two-line flavour each; the selection is the largest element on the register form. Secondary: the switch link (login ↔ register). Errors from the action render inline under the field they concern (`username taken`, `bad credentials`); network errors as a single `danger` line above the button. No password rules beyond 8..128 are displayed until violated.

**20.9.2 World** (`/play`). The canvas is the screen. HUD per §20.6. DOM elements present: a 56 px top bar with the nav (`Party · Box · Dex · Shop · Social · Guild · Market · Pass · Settings`, `NavLink`s with unread counts), the money counter (mono, tabular), the trainer level pip; the chat input (hidden until `T`); toasts bottom-right; `DialogueBox` bottom-centre (`--t-body-lg`, speaker in display face, `Enter`/`E` advances, ≤ 3 lines visible, remaining count shown). Empty: a zone with no other players shows nothing extra. Loading: `BootScreen` (§20.10.4) only before the first `/me`; a zone change shows only the banner. Error: `ConnectionOverlay` after 2 s disconnected (§20.3.4).

**20.9.3 Party** (`/play/party`). Left 60 %: six `CreatureCard variant="row"` in slot order (sprite, name/nickname, level, `HpBar`, status, held item icon), keyboard-reorderable (`Alt+↑/↓`) and pointer-draggable; reorder commits with `POST /party/reorder` on drop (optimistic order, revert on error). Right 40 %: the selected creature's detail — stats (`calcStat` from core for display, marked "at level"), four `MoveButton`s with PP, ability + held item selects (`POST /creature/:id/setup`), nickname (`POST /creature/:id/nickname`), favourite/lock toggles (`POST /creature/:id/flags`), release (`danger`, confirm modal, refused when locked). Empty: impossible (party ≥ 1). Loading: none (store). Error: toast.

**20.9.4 Box** (`/play/box`). A 10-column tile grid (`CreatureCard variant="tile"`, 72 px) paged 60 per page (`?page=`), with a sticky filter row (search by name/species, type, sort by level/dex/recent). The party strip is pinned at the top of the panel so deposit/withdraw is one drag or two clicks (`POST /party/withdraw`/`deposit`, `POST /party/swap` when the party is full and a slot is targeted). Selecting a tile shows the same detail column as Party. Empty: "Your box is empty — creatures you catch with a full party arrive here." Loading: skeleton grid. Error: toast + retry button in the panel header.

**20.9.5 Dex** (`/play/dex`) — *witnessing framing*. Header: "Witnessed **N / 130**" in display face with a segmented bar (caught solid, seen-only hatched, unknown empty) — this is the theme's win condition, so it is the largest number in the UI. Below: a 6-column grid in `dexNumber` order; unknown species render as a dark silhouette with `#???`; seen species show the sprite desaturated with the name; caught species show full colour with variant chips (up to 4 palette dots) and an alpha mark. Filters in the URL (`?type=&state=caught|seen|unknown&q=`). Selecting an entry opens a right detail: flavour text, types, evolution chain (arrows; unknown stages silhouetted), zones where recorded (from `dexEntries` caught zone), and the line "Recorded — this one cannot be quietly lost." for caught entries versus "Seen, not yet recorded." for seen. Empty: never (130 rows). Loading: skeleton grid. Error: panel retry.

**20.9.6 Shop** (`/play/shop`). Two tabs (`?tab=buy|sell`). Buy: a `Table` of stock (icon, name, one-line effect, price mono) with a quantity stepper (1..99) and a running total against the money counter; the row's buy button is disabled with the caption "Not enough" when `qty × price > money`. Sell: the inventory filtered to `sellPrice > 0`. Each transaction is one `useFetcher` action; the response is applied through `applyInventoryDelta`. Empty: "Nothing to sell." Loading: skeleton table. Error: 404 → "There is no shop here" (the panel still opens, so the URL is honest); other → retry.

**20.9.7 Social / DM** (`/play/social`, `/play/social/:username`). Left 320 px: three tabs (`Friends · Requests (n) · Nearby`); friends show online state (from `GET /friends`, refreshed on focus) and unread DM counts; each row has `Message · Challenge · Trade` (the latter two enabled only when the player is in this zone: `room.state.players.has(id)`, read via `useRoomState`). Right: the DM thread (`GET /messages/:username?limit=50`, older loaded on scroll-top with `?before=`), composer at the bottom (`POST /messages/:username`, 1..300 chars, counter visible from 250). Incoming DMs arrive as the `30-server-netcode.md` room message `dm` → `notifications.unreadDm` + thread append when open. Empty: friends "No friends yet — add someone by username" with the request field focused; thread "Say hello." Loading: skeleton rows. Error: retry.

**20.9.8 Guild** (`/play/guild`). No guild: search (`?q=`) + create form (name 3..24, tag `[A-Z0-9]{3,4}` validated live, the 5 000 founding cost shown on the button and disabled with "Not enough" below it) side by side. <!-- reconciled 2026-09-18: tag {3,4}, cost 5000 --> In a guild: header (name, tag, member count, MOTD editable by leader/officers), a `Table` of members (name, role, trainer level, last seen) with role actions gated by the viewer's role (`kick`/`promote`), and a `Leave` button (`danger`, confirm; leaders must promote first). Empty search: "No guilds match." Loading: skeleton. Error: retry.

**20.9.9 Marketplace** (`/play/market`, `/play/market/:listingId`). Filter bar bound to URL params (`search, variant, isAlpha, levelMin, levelMax, sort, page`); results as `Table` rows (sprite, species/level, variant/alpha chips, current bid, buy-now, time left mono, seller). Tabs `Browse · My listings · My bids`. The detail route shows the creature card, full bid history, a bid field pre-filled with `max(startingBid, currentBid + 50)` and buttons `Bid` / `Buy now` / (own) `Cancel`; list-from-box modal picks a box creature, starting bid ≥ 100, optional buy-now, duration `12 | 24 | 48` h; fee 5 % shown before confirm. Rate-limit responses (`5/h list`, `30/h bid`) render as the exact remaining-time caption. Empty: "No listings match — widen the filters." Loading: skeleton rows; the previous page stays visible under a 0.6 opacity while a new page loads. Error: retry.

**20.9.10 Battle Pass** (`/play/pass`). Header: trainer level (display face) and a progress bar to the next 1 000 exp with the exact `exp / 1000` in mono. A horizontal track of 100 reward tiles (virtualised, 10 visible), each `claimable · claimed · locked`; claimable tiles pulse once (160 ms) on entry. Level 50 shows the mythical choice as two `CreatureCard variant="showcase"` (`lumyth / noxmyth`) with "Choose once" — `POST /battle-pass/mythical-choice`. Claim → `POST /battle-pass/claim` → `applyInventoryDelta`. Empty: never. Loading: skeleton track. Error: retry.

**20.9.11 Settings** (`/play/settings`). Five tabs (`?tab=graphics|audio|accessibility|controls|social`). Every control is a `Toggle`/`Slider`/`Table` from §20.2.4 bound to `useSettingsStore`; changes apply immediately (no Save button) and the graphics tab shows the live `renderer.stats()` (draw calls, GPU ms) under the toggles. Accessibility tab first item: the reduced-motion tri-state with the current system value shown. Controls: the remap table (§20.8.5). Empty/loading: none. Error: none (local).

**20.9.12 Admin** (`/admin`). Not a `/play` child (no canvas; admins moderate from a plain page). Stats cards (total, banned, verified, online), a player search `Table` (`?q=&page=`) with `Ban`/`Unban` actions requiring a reason (1..200 chars). 403 → redirect to `/play`. Empty: "No players match." Loading: skeleton. Error: retry.

**20.9.13 Empty, loading and error states — the shared vocabulary**

| State | Component | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| Loading (first) | `Skeleton` blocks matching the final layout (rows/tiles/table), `--bg-2` with a 1.2 s shimmer (0 under reduced motion) | Shown only when `useNavigation().state === 'loading'` and no prior data; never a spinner overlay; never over the canvas |
| Loading (refresh) | previous content at opacity 0.6 | Filter/page changes |
| Empty | `EmptyState { icon, title, hint, action? }` — title `--t-h3`, hint `--t-body` in `--text-2`, one optional `Button` | Every list/table/grid passes `empty` explicitly; no silent blank panels |
| Error (route) | `RouteError` — title from `isRouteErrorResponse(err) ? err.status : 'Something broke'`, detail line, `Retry` (`useRevalidator().revalidate()`), `Back to world` | Rendered by the child route's `errorElement`, inside the panel |
| Error (action) | inline line under the control or a `danger` toast (`ttlMs` 6 000) | Field-specific when the server names a field |
| Offline | `ConnectionOverlay` (full-panel scrim, not over the canvas HUD) | After 2 000 ms in `'reconnecting'`; attempt counter visible |

---

## §20.10 PERFORMANCE RULES

### 20.10.1 Client-owned frame budget (main thread, ms per frame at 60 Hz; render's own 10.0 ms budget is in `PLAN.md`/`50-verification-budget.md`)

| Stage | Budget | Measured how |
|---|---|---|
| `input.sample + step` (prediction, ring buffer, message send every 3rd step) | 0.30 | `performance.now()` bracket, dev HUD p95 |
| `remotes.step` (≤ 48 actors interpolated) | 0.25 | same |
| `battle.player.step` | 0.30 | same |
| `audio.step` | 0.10 | same |
| `hudWriter.flush` (dirty fields only) | 0.20; 0.00 when the mask is 0 | same |
| React commit work on the 60 Hz path | **0.00** — asserted | `React.Profiler` in dev: any commit whose trigger stack includes `World3D.tick` fails the perf test |
| **Total client-owned** | **≤ 1.5 ms p95**, ≤ 3.0 ms p99.9 | `test/perf/tick.test.ts` over a 600-frame headless run with 48 remotes and a battle turn playing |

### 20.10.2 Rules

1. **No React on the 60 Hz path.** `World3D.tick` and everything it calls may not import `react`, `react-dom`, `react-router` or any `use*Store` hook (ESLint `no-restricted-imports` block scoped to `src/scene/**`, `src/battle/TimelinePlayer.ts`, `src/audio/**`). Stores are read via `getState()` and written only at the cadences named in §20.3–§20.6 (log ≤ 2 Hz, fps 1 Hz, connection on change).
2. **No per-frame allocation in input/prediction/interpolation.** `LocalPlayerController`, `RemoteInterpolator`, `HudWriter.flush` and `TimelinePlayer.step` are covered by `test/perf/alloc.test.ts`: a 600-step run with `--expose-gc` measures `process.memoryUsage().heapUsed` deltas between forced GCs and asserts ≤ 64 KB total growth (jitter allowance), and the source files are grepped for `new `, `[]`, `{}`, `.map(`, `.filter(`, `...` inside functions named `step|sample|flush|reconcile` — a hit fails the test unless the line carries `// alloc-ok: <reason>` (≤ 3 such lines per file).
3. **HUD dirty-flag.** `HudWriter.flush` calls `renderer.hud.set` with a partial containing only fields whose dirty bit is set; the render side redraws only those regions. Test: 600 idle frames (no input, no chat, no timeOfDay tick) produce exactly 0 `hud.set` calls; walking produces ≤ 2 per second (prompt changes) + nameplates at 2 Hz.
4. **Selectors return primitives.** `useRoomState` and every Zustand selector return primitives or shallow tuples; a Schema instance or store slice object is never returned (rule in §20.3.1/§20.3.3).
5. **Loaders are cheap or skeletons.** ≤ 400 ms p95 (§20.1.3); `shouldRevalidate: () => false` on `/play`; child loaders revalidate only on their own navigation or explicit `useRevalidator`.
6. **Images and sprites for the DOM** (`CreatureCard` thumbnails, dex grid) are the pipeline's 128 px WebP thumbnails from `40-pipelines.md` (`public/creatures/thumbs/<id>_front.webp` = `ManifestSpriteV2.thumb`, ≤ 6 KB each), `loading="lazy"`, `decoding="async"`; never the game atlases. <!-- reconciled 2026-09-18: path per 40-pipelines.md §40.6 -->
7. **Bundle**: the `/play` route imports `@ewa/render` and `three/webgpu` through `lazy` route modules so `/login` costs no Three.js. Shell (React + router + zustand + stores + ui) ≤ 180 KB gzipped; `/play` chunk (render + content + core + protocol + sdk) ≤ 900 KB gzipped; `vite build` fails via `build.chunkSizeWarningLimit` treated as an error in `vite.config.ts` when exceeded.

### 20.10.3 Chunked asset preload — first-frame budget 2.5 s on the slice

Target: a fresh `/play` load on Mirevale, cold cache, 50 Mbit/s, RTX 3060-class desktop, reaches the **first rendered world frame at ≤ 2 500 ms** from navigation start (`performance.mark('ewa:first-frame')` in `World3D.tick` after the first `renderer.frame`). The Playwright budget test asserts it on every CI run against the built client + a local server.

| Phase | Payload | Budget (cumulative) |
|---|---|---|
| 0 HTML + shell JS + `tokens.css` + 3 fonts (preloaded) | ≤ 180 KB + 360 KB | 450 ms |
| 1 `/play` chunk + `GET /me`, then `POST /zone/enter` + `consumeSeatReservation` (§20.3.4) | ≤ 900 KB JS; three round trips, the last two overlapping phase 2 | 1 100 ms |
| 2 zone materials for Mirevale — the **`field` tier** (512² KTX2 albedo/ORM/normal per surface, `10-render.md` §10.2.1): 15 surfaces × 3 × ≤ 0.10 MB | ≤ 6 MB (Mirevale 4.5) | 1 900 ms |
| 3 creature atlases for Mirevale's bind set (`manifest.zones.mirevale`, ≤ 8 × 2048² WebP with mips) + party singles + the player body GLB | ≤ 4 MB (a 14-species zone is 4.1 MB cold, 3.8 with the GLB cached) | 2 350 ms |
| **first frame** (zone built, DOF/bloom compiled, HUD fonts ready) | | **≤ 2 500 ms** |
| 4 (after first frame) audio manifest + zone theme stems + ui sfx | ≤ 5 MB | music starts ≤ 4 000 ms; the world is silent, not blocked |
| 5 (idle) **`hero` tier** of the current zone's `hero: true` surfaces (`renderer.materials.preloadHero`, ≤ 12 MB, swapped in place), then the neighbouring zone (`mire_route`) field materials + atlases | ≤ 22 MB | streamed at ≤ 2 MB/s in 256 KB `fetch` ranges so it never competes with a handover |

<!-- reconciled 2026-09-18: 40-pipelines.md OQ-2 — phase 2 keeps its 6 MB by loading the field tier; hero textures arrive in phase 5 -->

Rules: phases 2–3 are issued as parallel `fetch`es with `priority: 'high'`; phase 4–5 with `priority: 'low'`. A phase exceeding 2× its budget logs a `warn` toast in dev. `BootScreen` shows the phase name and a real byte-progress bar (from `Content-Length`), display face, no fake progress. Handover to a preloaded zone (phase 5 complete) must render its first frame within 250 ms of `handoverAck`; to a non-preloaded zone within 1 500 ms, with the HUD banner and the old zone still visible under the camera's 600 ms travel (spine §4.6 "no visible hitch" is measured as: no frame > 33 ms during the crossing).

### 20.10.4 `BootScreen` and the dev HUD

`BootScreen` is the `hydrateFallbackElement` of `/play`: key art, the phase label (`Waking the grove…` is not permitted — labels are literal: "Loading materials 3.1 / 6.0 MB"), the byte bar. It is replaced by the canvas the moment `/me` resolves; phases 2–3 continue behind a 400 ms fade of the same screen drawn *by the canvas HUD* so the DOM boot screen is gone before the first frame. The dev HUD (`F3`, `import.meta.env.DEV` or `?dev=1`) shows fps, frame ms p95, draw calls, GPU ms, texture MB, remote count, prediction error, rubber-bands, desync counter, audio voices, heap MB.

---

## NON-NEGOTIABLES (this file)

1. `GameCanvas` mounts once per session in a pathless layout above `/play`; no route, loader, error boundary or Suspense sits between it and the router root.
2. `applyProgressionDelta` and `applyInventoryDelta` are the only writers of progression and inventory; REST responses are mapped into those two shapes; `BattleEvent`s write nothing.
3. `room.state` is read in place via `useRoomState` (primitives only) or inside `World3D.tick`; it is never copied into a Zustand store.
4. Only authoritative `battleEvents.events` are ever played; the local `resolveTurn` preview is prewarm + digest only; a digest mismatch sends `battleResync`.
5. HP shown anywhere comes from `hpAfter`; the client never computes damage.
6. `World3D.tick` performs no React state write above the cadences in §20.3–§20.6 and no allocation in `step/sample/flush/reconcile`.
7. Remote avatars are interpolated from a 100 ms buffer with linear interpolation; exponential smoothing does not exist in `src/scene`.
8. Effective reduced motion removes shake, hitstop, slow-mo, chroma, grain and DOF and cannot be overridden; timelines differ between modes only in those frame kinds.
9. One token set, three self-hosted OFL fonts, 12 px caption / 14 px body minimums, zero inline styles (`ewa/no-inline-style`), CSS Modules only.
10. Every screen has explicit empty, loading and error states; no spinner ever covers the canvas.

## CUT DELIBERATELY (this file)

- **React Three Fiber / any React reconciler over the scene.** React ends at the canvas element.
- **vanilla-extract, Tailwind, styled-components.** Not installed; CSS Modules cover the need with zero dependencies.
- **URL state for modals and the battle.** Modals are `ui.modal`; a battle is `ui.battle`; refresh during a battle re-joins via `battleResync`, not via a `/battle/:id` route.
- **Mobile layouts, touch controls, responsive breakpoints.** Desktop only; below 1280 px the app shows a static message.
- **Client-side damage, catch, exp or badge logic.** `core` runs on the client for validation (`validateAction`) and preview digests only.
- **Custom smoothing/"rubber-band" heuristics.** Snap above 4 u, replay otherwise, 100 ms render blend; nothing else.
- **Per-frame HUD rebuilds and DOM HUDs.** The HUD is canvas, written through a dirty mask.
- **Music `loop = true` on sources.** Stems are double-scheduled to stay sample-aligned.
- **Voice-over, dialogue audio, lip sync.** Ducking exists for future VO and for modal focus; no VO ships.
- **Service worker / offline mode.** Preload is `fetch` with priority hints and the browser cache; no SW.

# EWAMON REMASTERED — SPEC §30: @ewa/server + NETCODE

**Status:** BINDING. Codes against `00-spine.md` and may not redefine anything in it. Where this file and `00-spine.md` disagree, the spine wins. Where `20-client.md` or `50-verification-budget.md` disagree with this file on a server behaviour, this file wins.

Verified against the installed `.d.ts`, not memory: `@colyseus/core 0.18.14` (`Room.d.ts`: `onCreate/onJoin/onLeave/onDispose/onAuth(client, options, context)`, `setTimestep(cb, delayMs)` with `setSimulationInterval` as its deprecated forwarding alias, `setFixedTimestep`, `onMessage(type, validationSchema: StandardSchemaV1, cb)`, `broadcast`, `allowReconnection(client, seconds)`, `setMetadata`, `lock/unlock`, `maxClients`, `patchRate`, `clock: ClockTimer`, `presence: Presence`; `MatchMaker.d.ts`: `query`, `findOneRoomAvailable`, `createRoom`, `reserveSeatFor(room: IRoomCache, options, authData)`, `getLocalRoomById`, `remoteRoomCall`, `defineRoomType`; `RegisteredHandler.d.ts`: `filterBy`, `sortBy`; `Transport.d.ts`: `Client.view?: StateView`, `AuthContext { token?, ip, headers }`; `IPC.d.ts`: `subscribeIPC`, `requestFromIPC`; `presence/Presence.d.ts`: `setex/get/del/hincrbyex/publish/subscribe`), `@colyseus/shared-types 0.18.2` (`ISeatReservation { name, sessionId, roomId, publicAddress?, processId?, reconnectionToken? }`), `@colyseus/schema 5.0.32` (`StateView.add/remove/dispose`), `@colyseus/ws-transport 0.18` (`WebSocketTransport({ pingInterval, pingMaxRetries, beforeUpgrade })`), `colyseus 0.18.5` (re-exports `RedisPresence`, `RedisDriver`), `@colyseus/sdk 0.18.2` (`consumeSeatReservation(ISeatReservation)`), `fastify 5.12.4`, `@fastify/jwt 10.2.2` (`FastifyJWTOptions { secret, sign: { expiresIn } }`, `request.jwtVerify()`, `reply.jwtSign()`), `@node-rs/argon2 2.2.1` (`hash(password, { memoryCost, timeCost, parallelism, algorithm: Algorithm.Argon2id })`, `verify`), `drizzle-orm 0.45.2` (`.for('update', { skipLocked: true })`), `pino 9.14.0`. `@fastify/rate-limit` is **not** installed; rate limiting is a server module (§30.6.4).

## §30.0 MODULE LAYOUT

`packages/server/src/`:

| File | Owns |
|---|---|
| `main.ts` | Boots one process: env parse → `createRepos` → Colyseus `Server` on `PORT_WS` → Fastify on `PORT_REST` → expiry/live-ops jobs. The only file with side effects at import time. |
| `env.ts` | `Env` (zod-parsed `process.env`, §30.1.2). Throws at boot on any invalid value. |
| `rooms/ZoneRoom.ts` | `ZoneRoom extends Room<{ state: ZoneState; metadata: ZoneMeta }>`. Tick, movement, AOI, handover, sharding. |
| `rooms/aoi.ts` | Pure `AoiGrid` (§30.2.5). Unit-tested without Colyseus. |
| `rooms/movement.ts` | Pure `validateInput` (§30.2.4). |
| `rooms/spawnDirector.ts` | Pure `SpawnDirector` (§30.3). |
| `rooms/battleSession.ts` | `BattleSession` + `BattleCoordinator` (§30.4). |
| `rooms/raidSession.ts` | `RaidSession` (§30.4.7). |
| `rooms/tradeSession.ts` | `TradeSession` (§30.8.3). |
| `rooms/challengeStore.ts` | Pure `ChallengeStore` (§30.4.5) — the legacy `createChallengeStore` shape, ported. |
| `rooms/storyBeats.ts` | Beat firing and completion on `interact` (§30.4.8); uses content's `nextStoryBeat`/`beatDoneFlag`/`harvestStateFor`. |
| `rooms/presenceStore.ts` | Pure `PresenceStore` (§30.7.4). |
| `rest/app.ts` | `buildRest(deps): FastifyInstance` — plugins, hooks, route registration. |
| `rest/routes/*.ts` | One file per row group of the §4.7 table. |
| `rest/auth.ts` | argon2id, JWT issue/verify, refresh rotation. |
| `rest/rateLimit.ts` | `RateLimiter` (§30.6.4). |
| `chat/filter.ts` | `censor`, `containsBlocked` over `chat/blocklist.ts` data (§30.6.5). |
| `liveops/*.ts` | Daily/weekly/battle-pass/swarm/raid scheduling (§30.9). |
| `jobs/marketExpiry.ts` | 60 s `closeExpired` loop (§30.8.2). |
| `metrics.ts`, `log.ts` | pino root logger, per-room counters (§30.10). |

Server code never imports `three`, `react`, `@ewa/render`, `@ewa/client` or `@colyseus/sdk` (eslint `ewa/server-side`). Server code never re-implements a formula that exists in `@ewa/core`; every battle number comes out of `resolveTurn`, every catch out of core, every Elo delta out of `eloDelta`.

---

## §30.1 PROCESS LAYOUT

### 30.1.1 One process, two listeners

```ts
// packages/server/src/main.ts — the shape, verbatim
import { Server, matchMaker, LocalPresence, LocalDriver } from '@colyseus/core';
import { WebSocketTransport } from '@colyseus/ws-transport';
import { RedisPresence, RedisDriver } from 'colyseus';
import Fastify from 'fastify';

export interface ServerDeps {
  readonly env: Env;
  readonly repos: ReturnType<typeof createRepos>;
  readonly content: ContentIndex;
  readonly log: Logger;               // pino
  readonly clock: () => number;       // Date.now, injected so tests can freeze it
}

export async function boot(deps: ServerDeps): Promise<{ colyseus: Server; rest: FastifyInstance; stop(): Promise<void> }>;
```

`boot`:

1. `presence = env.REDIS_URL ? new RedisPresence(env.REDIS_URL) : new LocalPresence()`; `driver = env.REDIS_URL ? new RedisDriver(env.REDIS_URL) : new LocalDriver()`.
2. `colyseus = new Server({ presence, driver, transport: new WebSocketTransport({ pingInterval: 5000, pingMaxRetries: 3, beforeUpgrade }), publicAddress: env.PUBLIC_ADDRESS, greet: false, gracefullyShutdown: true })`. `beforeUpgrade` returns `new Response(null, { status: 429 })` when the connecting IP has more than 8 open sockets (counted in `PresenceStore`, §30.7.4), and `403` when the IP is on the in-memory ban cache (§30.6.7).
3. `matchMaker.defineRoomType('zone', ZoneRoom).filterBy(['zoneId', 'shard'])` — `zoneId` and `shard` are `onCreate` options and metadata fields (§30.2.6). No other room type exists: battles, raids and trades are sub-states of `ZoneRoom`.
4. `await colyseus.listen(env.PORT_WS)`.
5. `rest = buildRest(deps)`; `await rest.listen({ port: env.PORT_REST, host: '0.0.0.0' })`.
6. Start `marketExpiry` (60 s), `liveOpsRollover` (checks every 60 s for a UTC day/week boundary), `presenceSweep` (30 s).
7. `stop()` = stop jobs → `rest.close()` → `matchMaker.gracefullyShutdown()`.

Dev ports: **2567** WebSocket, **4000** REST. The Vite client proxies `/api` → `:4000` and connects Colyseus to `ws://localhost:2567` directly. Production puts both behind one TLS terminator; the ports are not exposed.

Multi-process: only when `REDIS_URL` is set. Every cross-room call in this file uses `matchMaker.remoteRoomCall` or `presence.publish`, never a direct `getLocalRoomById` without a local fallback check, so a room on another process behaves identically. `getLocalRoomById` is used only as a fast path guarded by `if (local) … else remoteRoomCall`.

### 30.1.2 Environment

```ts
export const EnvSchema = z.object({
  NODE_ENV: z.enum(['development', 'test', 'production']).default('development'),
  PORT_WS: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(65535).default(2567),
  PORT_REST: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(65535).default(4000),
  PUBLIC_ADDRESS: z.string().min(1).optional(),                 // host[:port] the SDK should reach; required when REDIS_URL is set
  DATABASE_URL: z.string().url().optional(),                    // absent → PGlite at DATA_DIR/pglite (dev/test only; refused in production)
  DATA_DIR: z.string().min(1).default('./.data'),
  REDIS_URL: z.string().url().optional(),
  JWT_SECRET: z.string().min(32),                               // ≥ 32 bytes; no default, boot fails without it
  JWT_ACCESS_TTL_S: z.coerce.number().int().min(60).max(3600).default(900),      // 15 min
  REFRESH_TTL_D: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(90).default(30),
  CORS_ORIGINS: z.string().default('http://localhost:5173'),   // comma-separated exact origins
  TRUST_PROXY: z.union([z.literal('false'), z.literal('loopback'), z.string()]).default('loopback'),
  SERVER_SEED: z.string().min(1).optional(),                    // world rng seed; absent → fnv1a32(`${processId}:${bootMs}`) logged at boot
  ZONE_CAPACITY: z.coerce.number().int().min(2).max(500).default(200),
  TICK_HZ: z.literal('20').default('20'),                       // not configurable in v1; present so the value is visible
  LOG_LEVEL: z.enum(['trace', 'debug', 'info', 'warn', 'error']).default('info'),
  ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_USERNAME: z.string().optional(),              // dev only: sets is_admin on this username at boot
});
export type Env = z.infer<typeof EnvSchema>;
```

`NODE_ENV=production` additionally requires `DATABASE_URL`, `PUBLIC_ADDRESS` when `REDIS_URL` is set, and refuses `ADMIN_BOOTSTRAP_USERNAME`.

---

## §30.2 ZoneRoom

### 30.2.1 Definition

```ts
export interface ZoneMeta { readonly zoneId: ZoneId; readonly shard: number }         // shard 0..
export interface ZoneJoinOptions { readonly zoneId: ZoneId; readonly shard?: number; readonly handover?: { readonly fromRoomId: string; readonly x: number; readonly z: number } }
export interface ZoneAuth { readonly playerId: string; readonly username: string; readonly isAdmin: boolean }
export interface ZoneUserData {
  readonly playerId: string;
  cell: readonly [number, number] | null;   // last AOI cell; null before first tick
  lastSeq: number;                          // last accepted input seq
  budgetMs: number;                         // §30.2.4 dt budget
  lastInputAt: number;                      // clock ms
  battleId: string | null;
  tradeId: string | null;
  limiter: RateLimiterState;                // per-message sliding windows (§30.6.4)
}

export class ZoneRoom extends Room<{ state: ZoneState; metadata: ZoneMeta; auth: ZoneAuth; userData: ZoneUserData }> {
  static readonly TICK_MS = 50;             // 20 Hz
  static readonly PATCH_MS = 50;            // one schema patch per tick
  static readonly DAY_LENGTH_MS = 48 * 60_000;
  zone!: ZoneDef;
  heightfield!: Heightfield;
  aoi!: AoiGrid;
  spawns!: SpawnDirector;
  battles!: BattleCoordinator;
  raid: RaidSession | null = null;
  trades!: Map<string, TradeSession>;
  challenges!: ChallengeStore;
  worldRng!: Rng;                           // createRng(`${SERVER_SEED}:${zoneId}:${shard}:${bootMs}`).fork() per subsystem
}
```

### 30.2.2 Lifecycle (verbatim order)

**`onCreate(options: ZoneJoinOptions)`**
1. `this.zone = ZONES_BY_ID[options.zoneId]` — throws (room creation fails) on an unknown id; content ids are validated at build, so this can only be a forged join option.
2. `this.heightfield = getHeightfield(options.zoneId)` — content's memoised `buildHeightfield` (`00-spine.md` §3.11); the first call per process costs ≤ 40 ms per zone, then 0.
3. `this.maxClients = env.ZONE_CAPACITY`; `this.patchRate = ZoneRoom.PATCH_MS`; `this.autoDispose = true`; `await this.setMetadata({ zoneId: options.zoneId, shard: options.shard ?? 0 })`.
4. `this.state = new ZoneState()`; `state.harvestState = zone.harvestState` (the content default only — harvest is per player, spine §3.5/§4.1; the room never changes it); `state.timeOfDay = ((clock() % DAY_LENGTH_MS) / DAY_LENGTH_MS)` (wall-clock-anchored so every shard of every zone agrees on the hour).
5. Seed: `const root = createRng(`${env.SERVER_SEED}:${zoneId}:${shard}:${clock()}`)`; `this.worldRng = root`; `this.spawns = new SpawnDirector(zone, table, root.fork())`; `this.battles = new BattleCoordinator(root.fork(), …)`.
6. Register every message in `CLIENT_MESSAGES` with `this.onMessage(name, schema, handler)` — zod v4 schemas implement Standard Schema, so the room rejects malformed payloads before the handler runs; a rejection is answered with `error { code: 'invalidPayload' }` by the room's `onUncaughtException`. No `'*'` handler exists.
7. `this.setTimestep((dt) => this.tick(dt), ZoneRoom.TICK_MS)`.

**`onAuth(client, options, context)`** — `context.token` is the access JWT (the SDK sends it as the auth token). Verify with the same `JWT_SECRET`/algorithm as REST (`HS256`, `aud: 'ewa'`, `iss: 'ewa-server'`). Reject (`ServerError(401)`) on invalid/expired token, on `banned_at !== null`, and when `options.zoneId` is gated (`zone.gate !== null`) and the player lacks that badge in `badges` — the badge check runs here so a player cannot enter a gated room by joining it directly. Return `{ playerId, username, isAdmin }`.

**`onJoin(client, options, auth)`**
1. Load `players` row (`repos.players.byId`) + party (`repos.creatures.party`). A player with zero party members is refused (`ServerError(409, 'noParty')`) — cannot happen after registration, guards a corrupt row.
2. Multi-tab collapse: if `state.players.has(auth.playerId)` — another socket of the same player is already in this room — the **older** client is sent `error { code: 'busy', detail: 'replaced' }` and `client.leave(4001)`; the new one takes over the existing `PlayerState` (position kept). Across rooms: `PresenceStore.enter(playerId, roomId)`; if the player is present in a different room, that room is told via `presence.publish('ewa:kick', { playerId, exceptRoomId })` and drops the older session. One avatar per player, everywhere, always.
3. Position: `options.handover` present and `fromRoomId` matches a pending handover for this player (§30.2.7) → `(x, z)` from the handover, else from the row when `row.zoneId === this.zone.id`, else the zone's spawn point `zone.spawn` (spine §3.1 `ZoneDef.spawn`; `mirevale` is `{ x: 0, z: 6 }`, the `players.z` default). The position is then **snapped**: `isWalkable(heightfield, x, z) ? (x, z) : nearestWalkable(heightfield, x, z, 8)` (spiral search, 0.5 m step). <!-- reconciled 2026-09-18: ZoneDef.spawn exists (OQ 4) -->
4. `const ps = new PlayerState(); ps.id = playerId; ps.name = username; ps.x = x; ps.z = z; ps.body = row.body; ps.guildTag = guildTag ?? ''; state.players.set(playerId, ps)`.
5. `client.view = new StateView(); client.view.add(ps)`; `client.userData = { playerId, cell: null, lastSeq: 0, budgetMs: 0, lastInputAt: clock(), battleId: null, tradeId: null, limiter: createLimiterState() }`. The first `tick` computes the cell and adds the 3×3 neighbourhood.
6. Send `toast { level: 'info', text: zone.name }` is **not** done here — the client reads `state.harvestState` and the zone name from content. Nothing is sent on join except the schema.
7. `repos.players.setPosition(tx, playerId, zone.id, x, z, angle)` only when the position came from step 3's fallback branches (row was for another zone, or snapped). Handover writes are the destination's job (§30.2.7).

**`onLeave(client, code)`**
1. If `code` is a consented leave (4000) or a handover-complete leave (4002): remove immediately.
2. Otherwise `await this.allowReconnection(client, 20)`; on success the same `PlayerState` continues (no position change); on timeout, remove.
3. Remove = `battles.forfeitIfActive(playerId, 'disconnect')` (§30.4.4 — a PvP battle is a forfeit; wild/trainer/league end with no rewards; a raid seat is vacated), `trades.cancelFor(playerId)`, `challenges.dropFor(playerId)`, `aoi.removeEntity(ps)` (which calls `view.remove` on every viewer), `state.players.delete(playerId)`, `client.view.dispose()`, `PresenceStore.leave(playerId, roomId)`, one `setPosition` write with the last authoritative pose.

**`onDispose()`** — `spawns.dispose()`, cancel clock timers, flush metrics. Rooms auto-dispose when empty (`autoDispose = true`) after Colyseus' default grace; a zone with zero players costs zero ticks.

### 30.2.3 Heightfield contract — it lives in `@ewa/content` (`00-spine.md` §3.11)

<!-- reconciled 2026-09-18: the verbatim block this section used to hold moved to the spine (a higher section may not define a content export); `water` was added there (roster-coverage A2). The decision stands: pure content function, one Float32Array for server and client -->
The render package cannot import content (eslint `ewa/render`: three only) and the server cannot import render. Both can import `@ewa/content`, so the walkable-surface function is the pure content export `buildHeightfield(zone)` / `getHeightfield(zoneId)` of spine §3.11, built from `ZoneDef.layoutSeed`; the client hands the object to render as a descriptor (data, not code), and the server samples the same arrays with `sampleHeight`, `isWalkable`, `isWater`, `nearestWalkable`, `playableBounds`. `validate.ts` writes every zone's `digest` into `dist/content-report.json` and the full fields into `dist/heightfields/<zoneId>.json`; `50-verification-budget.md` §50.4.2 rebuilds all 21 in Node and in a browser worker and asserts equal digests. The server and the client therefore agree on walkability to the sample. **Zone bounds for movement are `playableBounds(h)`** — the field shrunk by the 16 m scenery margin: town ±64, route ±104, story ±84 (spine §3.1 extents).

### 30.2.4 Movement validation (pure)

```ts
// packages/server/src/rooms/movement.ts — verbatim
import { MOVEMENT } from '@ewa/protocol';   // 20-client.md §20.4.5: the ONE constants table for prediction and validation (reconciled 2026-09-18)
export const WALK_SPEED = MOVEMENT.walkSpeed;   // 4.2 m/s
export const RUN_SPEED = MOVEMENT.runSpeed;     // 7.0 m/s
export const SPEED_TOLERANCE = 1.15; // +15 %
export const MAX_WALK_STEP = WALK_SPEED * SPEED_TOLERANCE;   // 4.83 m/s
export const MAX_RUN_STEP = RUN_SPEED * SPEED_TOLERANCE;     // 8.05 m/s
export const BUDGET_MIN_MS = -100;   // an input may not spend more than 100 ms of time the server has not seen
export const BUDGET_MAX_MS = 250;    // idle time does not bank more than 250 ms

export type InputVerdict =
  | { readonly ok: true; readonly x: number; readonly z: number; readonly angle: number; readonly running: boolean; readonly budgetMs: number }
  | { readonly ok: false; readonly reason: 'staleSeq' | 'dtBudget' | 'speed' | 'unwalkable' | 'bounds' | 'inBattle'; readonly budgetMs: number };

export function validateInput(
  prev: { readonly x: number; readonly z: number; readonly lastSeq: number; readonly budgetMs: number; readonly inBattle: boolean },
  input: z.infer<typeof InputMsg>,            // { seq, x, z, angle, running, dtMs }
  h: Heightfield,
): InputVerdict;
```

Rules, in this order (first failure wins):

1. `seq <= prev.lastSeq` → `staleSeq` (replay or reorder; dropped silently — no snap, no error, the ack will carry the real `seq`).
2. `prev.inBattle` → `inBattle` (position is frozen during a battle; see §30.4.1).
3. `budget = prev.budgetMs - dtMs`; if `budget < BUDGET_MIN_MS` → `dtBudget`. The tick adds `TICK_MS` to every client's budget and clamps at `BUDGET_MAX_MS`, so the sum of accepted `dtMs` can never exceed real elapsed time by more than 100 ms. This is what closes the "send dt = 100 at 30 Hz" speed hack.
4. `dist = hypot(x - prev.x, z - prev.z)`; `cap = (running ? MAX_RUN_STEP : MAX_WALK_STEP) * dtMs / 1000`; `dist > cap + 0.02` → `speed` (0.02 m absorbs float32 quantisation on the wire).
5. `!isWalkable(h, x, z)` → `unwalkable`. Additionally the segment `prev → new` is sampled every 0.25 m; any unwalkable sample → `unwalkable` (no tunnelling through a 0.5 m wall in one 8 cm step is possible at these caps, but the check costs nothing).
6. Outside the bounds of §30.2.3 → `bounds`.
7. Otherwise `ok`; `angle` is wrapped into `[-π, π)`; `budgetMs = budget`.

Application (`ZoneRoom.onInput`): on `ok`, write `ps.x/z/angle/running`, `ps.moving = dist > 0.001`, `ps.seq = input.seq`, `userData.lastSeq = seq`. On any failure other than `staleSeq`, the server does **not** move the avatar and sets `ps.seq = input.seq` anyway — the client's reconciler (`20-client.md`) sees a `seq` acknowledged against an unchanged authoritative `x/z` and snaps to it. That single mechanism is the "ack with authoritative pose"; there is no separate correction message. `speed`, `unwalkable` and `bounds` rejections increment `metrics.rejectedInputs[reason]`; 30 rejections within 10 s from one client → `client.leave(4003)` and an `audit_log` row `movement.kick`.

`Room.input`/`defineInput` (Colyseus' built-in input buffer) is **not** used in v1: the message path above is validated by zod and by `validateInput`, and prediction runs against the `seq` echo. `setFixedTimestep` is likewise not used — 20 Hz `setTimestep` with the budget rule above is sufficient for a turn-based game whose only real-time element is walking.

### 30.2.5 AOI — `StateView` per client (the spine's §4.2 rule, implemented)

Cell size **32 units**, neighbourhood **3×3** (the spine fixes these; the earlier 24 m / 5×5 proposal is superseded).

```ts
// packages/server/src/rooms/aoi.ts — verbatim. No Colyseus import: `Viewer` is structural.
export const AOI_CELL = 32;
export interface Viewer { add(e: object): void; remove(e: object): void }
export interface AoiEntity { readonly id: string; x: number; z: number }
export type Cell = readonly [number, number];
export function cellOf(x: number, z: number): Cell;             // [Math.floor(x / AOI_CELL), Math.floor(z / AOI_CELL)]
export function neighbours(c: Cell): readonly Cell[];           // the 9 cells, sorted row-major

export class AoiGrid<E extends AoiEntity> {
  /** Entity moved or was created: updates its cell; returns the viewers whose 3x3 it entered/left. */
  place(e: E): { readonly entered: readonly string[]; readonly left: readonly string[] };   // viewer ids
  removeEntity(e: E): readonly string[];                        // viewers that could see it
  /** Viewer moved: returns entities newly inside / no longer inside its 3x3. */
  moveViewer(viewerId: string, from: Cell | null, to: Cell): { readonly show: readonly E[]; readonly hide: readonly E[] };
  removeViewer(viewerId: string): void;
  entitiesNear(c: Cell): readonly E[];                          // 3x3 union, for interact/engage range checks
  readonly stats: { cells: number; entities: number; viewers: number };
}
```

Tick order for AOI (after movement, before spawns):

1. For each client with an accepted move this tick: `to = cellOf(ps.x, ps.z)`; if `to` differs from `userData.cell`: `{ show, hide } = aoi.moveViewer(playerId, from, to)`; `for e of show: client.view.add(e)`; `for e of hide: client.view.remove(e)`; `userData.cell = to`. Own `PlayerState` is never removed (it was added in `onJoin` and `moveViewer` excludes the viewer's own entity).
2. For each entity (player or spawn) whose cell changed: `{ entered, left } = aoi.place(e)`; the corresponding clients' views get `add`/`remove`.
3. New spawn: `aoi.place(spawn)` then `view.add` on every viewer returned; `state.spawns.set(id, spawn)` **after** the adds. Despawn: `view.remove` on every viewer from `aoi.removeEntity(spawn)`, then `state.spawns.delete(id)`.

Only `players` and `spawns` are `@view()`-tagged; `battles`, `raid`, `tick`, `timeOfDay`, `weather`, `harvestState` broadcast to all. A client therefore receives per tick: its own row, ≤ N₉ player rows and ≤ S₉ spawn rows where N₉/S₉ are the 3×3 populations, plus the small broadcast set. The netcode harness (§30.10.3) asserts this shape.

### 30.2.6 Capacity and sharding

`maxClients = ZONE_CAPACITY` (200). Join flow, in the REST layer's `POST /zone/enter { zoneId }` (spine §4.7; the client calls it before every first Colyseus join and after a reload, using `GET /me`.presence.zoneId; it answers 403 `gated` when the zone's `gate` badge is missing and 409 `inBattle` during a battle): <!-- reconciled 2026-09-18: OQ 3 — both the route and the `presence` field exist -->

1. `rooms = await matchMaker.query({ name: 'zone', metadata: { zoneId } })` (typed `query<ZoneRoom>`), filtered `clients < maxClients && !locked`, sorted by `metadata.shard` ascending.
2. Pick the first; if none, `await matchMaker.createRoom('zone', { zoneId, shard: (max existing shard) + 1 })`.
3. `return { seat: await matchMaker.reserveSeatFor(room, { zoneId, shard }, /* authData */ null) }` → the `ISeatReservation` handed to the client as the spine's `SeatReservation` (`{ name, sessionId, roomId, publicAddress?, processId? }`), which it passes to `consumeSeatReservation` unchanged.

A shard is a full, independent instance of the zone (own spawns, own raid). Players on different shards of the same zone do not see each other; friends see `presence.roomId`. Shard 0 is preferred for every join, so shards ≥ 1 drain and auto-dispose as population falls. Guild/party co-location is a follow-up (cut deliberately, see end).

### 30.2.7 Handover — zero visible hitch

Links are `zone.links[dir]`. The link edge is the field boundary on that side; the trigger line is **6 m** inside the edge.

Source room, in `tick`, for each player not in a battle/trade whose accepted position crosses a trigger line for direction `d` and who has no pending handover:

1. `dest = zone.links[d]`; if `DEST.gate` is a badge the player lacks (checked against a badge set cached in `userData` at join and refreshed by `progressionDelta`): send `toast { level: 'warn', text: 'The way is barred. Badge required: <name>' }`, and push the player back 1.0 m along `−d` (authoritative pose changes; the `seq` echo snaps the client). No handover.
2. Otherwise reserve: `room = await pickZoneRoom(dest)` (§30.2.6 steps 1–2, on the destination's shard 0 by preference); `seat = await matchMaker.reserveSeatFor(room, { zoneId: dest, handover: { fromRoomId: this.roomId, x: x', z: z' } }, null)`, where `(x', z')` is the entry point on the destination's opposite edge: same lateral coordinate, 6.5 m inside the destination edge, snapped to `nearestWalkable`.
3. `presence.setex(`ewa:handover:${playerId}`, JSON.stringify({ fromRoomId: this.roomId, toRoomId: seat.roomId, sessionId: seat.sessionId, x: x', z: z', at: clock() }), 10)`.
4. `client.send('handover', { roomId: seat.roomId, seat, zoneId: dest, x: x', z: z' })`. `seat` is the whole `ISeatReservation` object — the spine's `SeatReservation` type (§4.4) — because `consumeSeatReservation` needs `name`, `sessionId`, `roomId`, and on multi-process `publicAddress`/`processId`. <!-- reconciled 2026-09-18: OQ 1 — the spine field was widened from string to SeatReservation; no JSON encoding -->
5. Mark `userData.handover = { toRoomId, sentAt }`; the avatar keeps ticking in the source (inputs still accepted for ≤ 5 s so the walk animation continues across the line).

Client (`20-client.md` §20.3.4): `destRoom = await sdk.consumeSeatReservation(seat)`; wait for the first `onStateChange`; send `handoverAck { roomId, seat: seat.sessionId }` to the **destination**; keep the source room open until the destination's state has arrived; then `sourceRoom.leave(true)` with code 4002.

Destination room, `onJoin` with `options.handover`: validates `presence.get('ewa:handover:<playerId>')` — must exist, `toRoomId === this.roomId`, `fromRoomId === options.handover.fromRoomId`, `sessionId === client.sessionId`. Any mismatch → `ServerError(403, 'badHandover')` (a forged handover option cannot teleport). On `handoverAck` from that client: `repos.players.setPosition(tx, playerId, dest, x', z', angle)` — **the one authoritative position write of a crossing**, then `presence.publish(`ewa:handover:done:${fromRoomId}`, { playerId })`, then `presence.del` the key.

Source room, on `ewa:handover:done` for a player it still holds, or 5 s after sending `handover` if nothing arrived: `onLeave`-style removal without a position write (removal code 4002 path). If the destination never confirms and the client is still connected to the source after 5 s, the source clears `userData.handover`, pushes the player 1.0 m back inside and lets them try again; the reservation expires by itself (Colyseus default seat reservation 15 s).

Invariant: at every instant the player exists in at least one room's `state.players` until the source removes them, and never in two rooms for more than 5 s. The position row is written exactly once per crossing, by the destination. Legacy fade-to-black (`sanitizeMapPosition` + full reload) does not exist here.

### 30.2.8 `tick(dtMs)` — verbatim order

```
1. state.tick += 1; state.timeOfDay = (clock() % DAY_LENGTH_MS) / DAY_LENGTH_MS
2. for each client: userData.budgetMs = min(BUDGET_MAX_MS, budgetMs + TICK_MS)
   (inputs are applied when they arrive, in onMessage('input'); the tick only refills budget)
3. emote timers: any PlayerState with emote !== 0 whose emoteSetAt + 2000 <= now → emote = 0
4. AOI pass (§30.2.5 steps 1–2)
5. spawns.tick(now, state.timeOfDay) → { spawned, despawned } → AOI step 3, state.spawns writes
6. handover triggers (§30.2.7)
7. battles.tick(now) — deadlines, auto-actions, resolution, persistence (§30.4.3); raid.tick(now)
8. trades.tick(now) — 120 s inactivity cancel
9. challenges.sweep(now) — 45 s TTL
10. metrics.tickMs = measured; if tickMs > 40 for 3 consecutive ticks → log warn 'tick overrun'
```

Step 7 may `await` (one DB transaction per resolved turn). The tick callback is `async`; Colyseus' `setTimestep` does not await it, so `battles.tick` must never resolve the same battle twice — `BattleSession.resolving: boolean` guards it, and a turn whose transaction is in flight when the next tick arrives is simply skipped that tick.

---

## §30.3 SPAWN DIRECTOR (server-owned wild spawns)

```ts
// packages/server/src/rooms/spawnDirector.ts — verbatim; pure, no Colyseus, no Date
export interface SpawnRecord {
  readonly id: string;              // `${zoneId}:${counter}` — counter is per-director, monotonic
  readonly speciesId: string; readonly level: number; readonly variantKey: VariantKey; readonly isAlpha: boolean;
  readonly ability: string;         // species.abilities[rng.int(abilities.length)] — one draw at spawn (spine §3.2, roster-coverage A3)
  readonly medium: 'land' | 'water';// EncounterSlot.medium ?? 'land'
  readonly x: number; readonly z: number;
  readonly bornAt: number;          // ms
  engagedBy: string | null;         // player uuid
  engagedAt: number | null;
  despawnAt: number;                // ms; extended by engagement
}
import { ROUTE_TUNING, TOWN_SPAWN_TUNING, STORY_TUNING, type SpawnTuning } from '@ewa/content';   // spine §3.2 — reconciled 2026-09-18: tunings are content data, one copy
/** Tuning by zone: route → ROUTE_TUNING; the 3 towns with a table (saltmourn · skyrend · solenne) → TOWN_SPAWN_TUNING; story → STORY_TUNING; a zone with encounterTable null runs no director. */
export function tuningFor(zone: ZoneDef): SpawnTuning | null;

export interface LiveOpsMods { readonly alphaBonus: number; readonly variantBonus: number; readonly swarmSpecies: readonly string[]; readonly swarmWeightBoost: number; readonly variantPool: readonly VariantKey[] }

export class SpawnDirector {
  constructor(zone: ZoneDef, table: EncounterTable | null, rng: Rng, h: Heightfield, tuning: SpawnTuning, content: ContentIndex);
  setLiveOps(m: LiveOpsMods): void;                                      // re-read from §30.9 at each rollover
  tick(now: number, timeOfDay: number): { readonly spawned: readonly SpawnRecord[]; readonly despawned: readonly string[] };
  engage(spawnId: string, playerId: string, now: number): 'ok' | 'notFound' | 'engaged' | 'tooFar';
  release(spawnId: string, outcome: 'caught' | 'ko' | 'fled' | 'timeout' | 'playerLeft', now: number): void;  // despawns for caught/ko/fled/timeout; frees for playerLeft
  get(spawnId: string): SpawnRecord | null;
  readonly all: readonly SpawnRecord[];
}
```

Rules:

- **Day/night.** `timeOfDay` in `[0.25, 0.75)` is day, else night (dawn at 06:00, dusk at 18:00 of the 48-minute day). A roll considers slots whose `timeOfDay` is `'any'` or matches. A spawn born in the day is not despawned at dusk; it lives out `lifetimeMs`.
- **Roll.** Weight-proportional pick over eligible slots (weights multiplied by `swarmWeightBoost` for `swarmSpecies`); `level = levelMin + rng.int(levelMax - levelMin + 1)`. One `rng.pick`-equivalent draw for the slot, one for the level.
- **Alpha.** `rng.next() < ALPHA_CHANCE + alphaBonus` where `ALPHA_CHANCE = 0.02` (content §3.2) — **not** 1/120: the content constant is the source of truth and 1/120 (0.0083) is the legacy value this replaces. A slot with `tier: 'alpha'` forces `isAlpha = true`. One draw.
- **Variant.** `rng.next() < VARIANT_CHANCE + variantBonus` (0.03 base) → `rng.pick(variantPool)`; `variantPool` is `WEEKDAY_VARIANT[weekday]` when non-null, else the 4 non-normal keys. Two draws when the first succeeds, one otherwise.
- **Position.** Up to 8 attempts: `x = originX + 1 + rng.next() * (size - 2)`, same for `z`, accepted if (`medium === 'water' ? isWater : isWalkable`) and ≥ 6 m from every other spawn and ≥ 10 m from every player. Failure → no spawn this tick. (The 16 m margin is `walkable = 0`, so spawns never land in scenery.)
- **Cadence.** When `all.length < targetCount` and `now >= nextSpawnAt`: spawn one, `nextSpawnAt = now + respawnMinMs + rng.int(respawnMaxMs - respawnMinMs + 1)`. At most one spawn per tick.
- **Despawn.** `now >= despawnAt` with `engagedBy === null`; engagement sets `despawnAt = max(despawnAt, now + engagedTimeoutMs)` so a 90 s stall (client vanished mid-fight) releases the creature for everyone. `SpawnState.despawnAtTick` mirrors `despawnAt` as a tick count so clients can fade it.
- **Engage.** `engageSpawn { spawnId }` from a client: requires no active battle, the spawn present in `aoi.entitiesNear(userData.cell)` (an out-of-AOI id → `notFound`), distance ≤ 3.0 m (`tooFar` → `error { code: 'notAllowed', detail: 'tooFar' }`), `engagedBy === null` (`engaged` → `busy`), and for a `medium: 'water'` spawn the player's own position on a `water` sample (`isWater(h, ps.x, ps.z)`; otherwise `notAllowed 'needSurf'` — standing on water requires the HM Surf key item, which the client checks before allowing the step onto a water-only sample and the server checks on `input`: a water-only sample is walkable for a player only when `inventory['HM Surf'] > 0`). Two players who send `engageSpawn` in the same tick for the same spawn: the first message processed wins; the second gets `error { code: 'busy' }`. Success → `SpawnState.engagedBy = playerId` (broadcast through AOI so others see it claimed) and `BattleCoordinator.startWild(...)` (§30.4.4).

The director's rng is `worldRng.fork()`; the sequence of spawns for a given `(SERVER_SEED, zoneId, shard, bootMs)` is reproducible in a test by driving `tick` with synthetic `now` values.

---

## §30.4 BATTLE SUB-STATE

### 30.4.1 Session

```ts
// packages/server/src/rooms/battleSession.ts — verbatim
export interface Seat { readonly side: 0 | 1; readonly playerId: string | null; readonly sessionId: string | null } // null player = wild/NPC/league side
export interface BattleSession {
  readonly id: string;                       // uuid
  readonly format: BattleFormat;
  readonly zoneId: ZoneId;
  readonly seats: readonly [Seat, Seat];
  state: BattleState;                        // core; the ONLY copy of the truth
  readonly seed: RngState;                   // createBattle's seed snapshot, for battle_history.seed
  readonly actionsLog: [Action | null, Action | null][];   // one entry per resolved resolveTurn, for battle_history.actions
  pending: [Action | null, Action | null];   // actions received for the CURRENT turn
  deadlineAt: number;                        // ms; 30 s after the turn opened
  resolving: boolean;
  readonly spawnId: string | null;           // wild: the SpawnRecord it consumes
  readonly npc: { readonly trainerId: string; readonly reward: number; readonly leagueId: string | null } | null;
  readonly beatId: string | null;            // §30.4.8: the story beat this battle completes on a side-0 win
  readonly staticSpecies: string | null;     // §30.4.6 `static`: the LegendaryStatic whose flag is set on win or catch
  readonly startedAt: number;
  readonly x: number; readonly z: number;    // where the arena dresses (BattleSummary)
}
export const TURN_DEADLINE_MS = 30_000;
export const REPLACEMENT_DEADLINE_MS = 20_000;
```

While `userData.battleId !== null`: `input` messages are rejected with `inBattle` (avatar frozen at the pre-battle pose; `PlayerState.inBattle = true`), `engageSpawn`/`challenge`/`tradeRequest`/`interact` are answered `error { code: 'busy' }`, handover triggers are skipped. Emote and chat still work.

### 30.4.2 Message → action

`battleAction { battleId, turn, action }` handler:

1. `session = battles.get(battleId)`; `!session || session.seats.every(s => s.playerId !== playerId)` → `error { code: 'notFound' }` (a forged battleId for someone else's battle leaks nothing — the same code as a non-existent id).
2. `turn !== session.state.turn + 1` (the turn being collected) → `error { code: 'notAllowed', detail: 'wrongTurn' }`. Replacement phases keep the same `turn`, so the client sends the same `turn` again with a `switch`.
3. `side = seat.side`; `err = validateAction(session.state, side, action)`; `err !== null` → `error { code: 'notAllowed', detail: err }`.
4. `session.pending[side] !== null` → `error { code: 'notAllowed', detail: 'alreadySubmitted' }` (no changing your mind).
5. `session.pending[side] = action`. If every seat that must act has acted → `resolveNow(session)`.

"Must act": in a replacement phase only the `mustSwitch` side(s); otherwise every seat with a non-null `playerId`. NPC/wild sides never act — core chooses via `chooseNpcAction` when handed `null`.

### 30.4.3 Turn pipeline (one transaction)

`resolveNow(session)`:

1. `session.resolving = true`. `const before = session.state; const result = resolveTurn(before, session.pending, content)` — pure; a thrown error here is a server bug (validateAction ran), logged at `error`, the battle is voided (`battleEnd` with `{ winner: 'draw' }` and no rewards) and `metrics.battleFaults += 1`.
2. `session.actionsLog.push(session.pending)`; `session.state = result.state`; `session.pending = [null, null]`; `deadlineAt = now + (anyMustSwitch ? REPLACEMENT_DEADLINE_MS : TURN_DEADLINE_MS)`.
3. **Persist**, one `db.transaction` — for each seat with a `playerId`, in ascending `playerId` order:
   a. `repos.players.lock(tx, playerId)`.
   b. For each battler on that side whose `creature.id` is a real row: `repos.creatures.update(tx, creature)` **only** for fields battle may change — `hp`, `moves[].pp`, `status`, `exp`, `level`, `heldItem` (consumed berry/policy → `null`) — read back from `result.state`. Creatures are projected from rows at battle start; the row is the truth between turns.
   c. Consumables used this turn (`itemUsed` events with `consumedHeld === false`): `repos.inventory.add(tx, playerId, item, -1)` — the balance was checked at `validateAction` time from a party/inventory snapshot refreshed every turn; a negative result throws and voids the battle (cannot happen without a concurrent REST use; then the battle is voided rather than the item duplicated).
   d. Orbs thrown (`catchThrow`): `inventory.add(tx, playerId, orb, -1)`.
   e. `catchResult { caught: true }`: `repos.creatures.insert(tx, wildCreature with ownerId = playerId, caughtAt: { zoneId, at: now }, 'box' or party slot if party.length < 6)`; `repos.progress.markCaught(...)` → `{ newSpecies }`; `addTrainerExp(catch + newSpecies ? catchNewSpecies : 0)`.
   f. `levelUp`/`expGain` are already in the creature rows (b). `evolveReady` and `learnMove` are events only; nothing persists (§2.16 — REST actions).
   g. On `battleEnd`: rewards (§30.4.4), `repos.history.recordBattle(tx, …)` per player seat, `audit 'battle.end'`; when `session.beatId !== null` and side 0 won, the beat completion of §30.4.8 step 4; when `session.staticSpecies !== null` (a `LegendaryStatic` battle) and the outcome is a win or a catch, `setFlags([static.flag])`.
   h. `repos.progress.markSeen` for every wild/NPC species revealed this turn (first `switchIn` of an opposing slot).
4. Send, **after** commit: to each player seat `battleEvents { battleId, turn: result.state.turn, events: result.events, stateDigest: digestBattleState(result.state), rng: result.state.rng }`. Then, when 3 changed anything, `inventoryDelta` (absolute counts for touched items; `creaturesUpdated` with the rows written; `creaturesAdded` on catch) and `progressionDelta` (dex/trainerExp/badges/rating/flags/quests as touched). Then `battleEnd { outcome, rewards }` when ended.
5. `BattleSummary.turn = state.turn`. On end: `state.battles.delete(id)`, `PlayerState.inBattle = false`, `userData.battleId = null`, `spawns.release(spawnId, outcomeToRelease)`, `session.resolving = false`, drop the session. Wild battle ended by `fled`/`ko`/`caught` → spawn despawns for everyone; the only case that frees a spawn is `playerLeft` before turn 1 resolves.

A transaction failure rolls everything back and the session keeps `before` as its state (step 2's assignment is applied only after commit succeeds — implement as `next = …; await tx; session.state = next`). The client's preview is then wrong; the digest mismatch on the next `battleEvents` forces a `battleResync`.

`battleResync { battleId }`: answers `battleResync { battleId, state: session.state, stateDigest }` only to a seat holder; the full `BattleState` for the opposing side includes its party — for **pvp** the opposing side's unrevealed party members are sent with `hp`, `moves`, `heldItem`, `ability` intact (Pokémon-style team preview is a client choice; hiding them would require a projection the spine does not define). Accepted for v1 — spine §4.5 (reconciled 2026-09-18).

**Deadline.** `battles.tick(now)`: for a session with `now >= deadlineAt && !resolving`: for each seat that must act and has `pending === null` — replacement phase → `switch` to the lowest-index `hp > 0` party member; otherwise `{ kind: 'move', move: firstMoveWithPp }` or, if no PP anywhere, `{ kind: 'move', move: active.moves[0].id }` (core's struggle rule, if any, is core's; the spine defines none, so `validateAction` fails on `noPp` and the fallback is `forfeit` for pvp and `flee` for wild — `flee` fails when trapped, in which case the wild side simply acts). Then `resolveNow`. Two consecutive deadline auto-actions by the same player in a pvp battle = forfeit.

### 30.4.4 Formats

| Format | Start | Side 1 | Rewards on side-0 win (`RewardsSummary`) | Elo |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `wild` | `engageSpawn` accepted | `kind: 'wild'`, one battler projected from the `SpawnRecord` (species default moves at that level via `learnableMoves`, `ability = record.ability` — rolled at spawn, spine §3.2 / roster-coverage A3, reconciled 2026-09-18 — `heldItem null`, `hp = maxHp`), `canFlee` on side 0, `canCatch` on side 0 | money 0; `trainerExp: TRAINER_EXP.wildWin` (+ `catch`/`catchNewSpecies` on catch; the catch ends the battle as `caught`, which is also a win for exp purposes) | — |
| `trainer` | `interact { kind: 'npc', targetId }` on a `TrainerDef` within 3 m; refused if `story_flags` has `trainer:<id>:beaten` and (`rematchDays === null` or fewer than `rematchDays` days since the flag was set) | `kind: 'npc'`, `trainerId = TrainerDef.id`, team from content (same projection rule) | `money: TrainerDef.reward` (rematch: `floor(reward / 2)`); `trainerExp: trainerWin`; sets flag `trainer:<id>:beaten`; quest `defeatTrainer`/`winBattles` advance | — |
| `league` | `interact { kind: 'gym', targetId: LeagueTrainer.id }` within 4 m of the gym marker; gyms require the previous badge in order (order 1 needs none); elite require all 8 badges and the previous elite (order); champion requires the 3 elite flags. Refused with `toast` when already beaten (no rematch in v1) | `kind: 'npc'`, `trainerId = LeagueTrainer.id`, team verbatim from content including `nickname/ability/heldItem` | `money: reward`; `badge: LeagueTrainer.badge` via `awardBadge` (audit `badge.award`); `trainerExp: gymWin/eliteWin/championWin` by stage; `setFlags(setsFlags)`; `championWin` additionally sets `champion:zeon:beaten` — the id is `'zeon'`, and the check anywhere in server code is `=== 'zeon'` | — |
| `pvp` | challenge handshake (§30.4.5) | `kind: 'player'` | `money 0`, `trainerExp: pvpWin` / `pvpLoss` to loser; `ratingDelta` from `eloDelta(winner.rating, loser.rating)` applied with `applyRating` to both, floor 0 (repo); `rankedMatches + 1`; draw → `eloDrawDelta` | yes |
| `raid` | §30.4.7 | shared boss `Side` | per participant: `trainerExp: raidClear`, `money: 50 * bossLevel`, catch window | — |

Side 0 is always the human player (pvp: the challenger). Losing a `trainer`/`league`/`wild` battle: every party creature keeps its post-battle `hp` (possibly 0); no money is lost (the legacy "blackout fee" is not carried); the client's `20-client.md` sends the player to the last healer on its own initiative via `POST /heal`, which requires being in a `healer: true` zone — so a wiped party in a route means walking back, which is the intended cost.

Exp share follows §2.16 (every participated non-fainted battler on the winning player side gets the full gain); core emits it; the server only persists rows.

### 30.4.5 PvP challenge handshake — presence re-checked before creation

